# game/ divergence census

Generated by `native/tools/divergence_census.py` — do not edit by hand. Regenerate with
`python3 -I native/tools/divergence_census.py` (`--fetch` to update the zeldaret/tww cache in
`build/upstream-tww.git`). Step G1 of [GAME_CODE_ORGANIZATION.md](GAME_CODE_ORGANIZATION.md).

Base: the import `ed1058e` (snrubrm/tww `b09eebc`); compared with HEAD, whose game/ is the tree `eaa0640`.
Upstream: zeldaret/tww main `208fefce0 2026-10-19 misc cleanup; reduce unneeded indentation`. The fork branched from zeldaret at `ae3b3b0de 2026-09-22 d_a_saku GLZE01 (#1214)`; since then zeldaret has 125 commits and the fork 2349.
Import equal to snrubrm `b09eebc` (src/, include/, LICENSE trees): **yes, verified**.

## Summary

| | |
| --- | --- |
| Files touched since the import | 599 (588 changed, 11 added), besides 240 files now equal to zeldaret/tww |
| Lines | +28267 / -9995 |
| Guarded blocks added (`#if` chains naming a guard macro) | 1606 (1602 outermost) |
| Lines inside the outermost added guarded blocks | 14541 |
| **Unguarded code divergence** (code changed outside any guard) | **489 added, 223 removed, in 93 files** |
| Unguarded annotations and cosmetic changes | 1904 added, 1657 removed, in 155 files |
| Unguarded lines that are zeldaret/tww's (headers partially merged with it) | 4306 added, 7616 removed, in 250 files |

Guard macros: `TARGET_PC`, `__MWERKS__`, `__clang__`, `TARGET_LITTLE_ENDIAN`, `COS_*`, `address_sanitizer` (in any `#if`/`#elif` of the chain). A line is guarded when it lies inside such a chain (the original code in the `#else` branch included). Unguarded added lines: added lines outside every guard chain. Unguarded removed lines: removed lines whose text (whitespace ignored) is not kept in a guarded branch. Blank lines are not counted. Unguarded lines are split into annotations and cosmetic changes (the same code on the GameCube once the identity macros of `game/include/helpers` — `BE()`, `LE()`, `BE_HOST()`, `RES_*()`, `OFFSET_PTR()`, `OFFSET_PTR_RAW` — are expanded and comments and whitespace are ignored; `#include "helpers/..."`; comment-only lines) and code divergence (everything else). Files equal to zeldaret/tww (step G4) are listed apart and left out of everything else; in the other files an unguarded line that zeldaret added as well (or a removed line that zeldaret removed as well) is counted as zeldaret's, not as our divergence.

Files added to game/ (ours, not decompiled code): `game/README.md` (+29), `game/UPSTREAM` (+9), `game/include/helpers/endian.h` (+26), `game/include/helpers/endian_gx.hpp` (+10), `game/include/helpers/endian_ssystem.h` (+10), `game/include/helpers/offset_ptr.h` (+21), `game/src/d/actor/d_a_dungeon_flag_sw.cpp` (+122), `game/src/d/actor/d_a_switch_op.cpp` (+256), `game/src/pal/d/actor/d_a_bst.inc` (+3210), `game/src/pal/d/d_file_select.inc` (+4642), `game/src/pal/d/d_menu_window.inc` (+2150).
